# AI03 evidence - approval path of a save_candidate candidate (E-AI03-11)
# Captured: 2026-10-29T11:44:33.1924993+05:30
# Repository HEAD: 1376af5 (working tree contains uncommitted AI03 changes)

== PURPOSE AND SCOPE ==
Verify that a candidate saved with the AI03 save_candidate tool reaches a plan only through the existing reviewer approval
(POST /api/drafts/{planningJobId}/approve with body {candidateIndex}), and that the approval keeps its server-side revalidation,
inventory reservation rule, locks, business-action record and idempotency. One bounded verification; no other AI requirement.
Model replies are MOCKED (fake HTTP handler). No real Ollama inference was run and the API was not started: the tests host the
planning and approval endpoints in-process (TestServer) over an isolated in-memory SQLite database seeded by DatabaseInitializer.
The original maintenance-planner.db and its -wal/-shm sidecars were not opened, read or modified.

== FILES CHANGED ==
tests/MaintenancePlanner.IntegrationTests/AI03ToolLoopIntegrationTests.cs:
  - JobFixture now also maps the existing approval endpoints (app.MapApprovalEndpoints()) and offers ApproveAsync / WithDatabaseAsync helpers.
  - New: SavedCandidate_IsApprovedOnlyByTheExplicitApprovalAction_WhichReservesLocksAndRecordsIt
  - New: SavedCandidate_ThatBecameStaleAfterSaving_IsRejectedAtApproval_AndNothingIsWritten (2 cases)
No application source code was changed in this stage.

== SCENARIO (as implemented in the tests) ==
1. A planning job runs through the production registration (AddOllamaPlanningInterpreter, AddPlanningTools); the scripted model calls
   save_candidate with a valid P-101 schedule starting 08:30 (differs from both scheduler candidates), then returns the final interpretation.
2. The saved candidate is the last entry of the job's unapproved candidates.
3. Success test: approve without an index (expect CANDIDATE_SELECTION_REQUIRED), then approve the saved candidate's index, then repeat it.
4. Stale tests: after saving, the isolated test database is changed (TECH-003 availability removed, or BR-101 on-hand set to 0), then the
   saved candidate is approved and must be rejected with nothing written.

  SNAPSHOT after save:      jobs=1;drafts=0;reservations=0;locks=0;edits=0;progress=0;actions=0;stock=BR-101:1.0/0.0
  SNAPSHOT after approval:  jobs=1;drafts=1;reservations=1;locks=3;edits=0;progress=0;actions=1;stock=BR-101:1.0/1.0
  RESULT: approve without index -> 409 CANDIDATE_SELECTION_REQUIRED; approve candidateIndex 2 (saved candidate) -> 202 /api/drafts/d06507bb-e190-4243-af4a-6bd15abce998; draft Approved, reservation BR-101 x1.0 Confirmed, 3 locks, action 'Approved' by 'unspecified'; repeat -> 202, no new rows

== BEHAVIOUR VERIFIED (by the assertions of the tests above, when they pass) ==
- Saving does not approve: after the job, drafts/reservations/locks/edits/progress/business actions are 0 and BR-101 stays 1.0/0.0; only the job row is new.
- Approval is explicit: without candidateIndex the endpoint returns 409 CANDIDATE_SELECTION_REQUIRED and writes nothing.
- Approving the saved candidate's index returns 202 with Location /api/drafts/{draftId}; exactly one Approved draft for the job whose
  ApprovedCandidatePayload is the saved schedule (first placement 08:30); one Confirmed BR-101 x1 reservation and BR-101 reserved 1;
  three locks (first technician per task); one 'Approved' business action ('Approved alternative N of N.').
- A repeated approval returns 202 and writes nothing more (existing idempotency).
- Revalidation at approval: a saved candidate whose technician lost availability is rejected (409 CANDIDATE_NO_LONGER_VALID, TECHNICIAN_UNAVAILABLE).
- Reservation rule at approval: a saved candidate whose part is no longer in stock is rejected (409 INSUFFICIENT_INVENTORY, BR-101).
- Rejected approvals leave drafts, reservations, locks, edits, progress, business actions and stock unchanged.

== LIMITATIONS AND UNVERIFIED SCENARIOS ==
- Mocked model only; no real-model run of save_candidate or of this approval path. No successful end-to-end real-model run exists (E-AI03-08).
- The test host registers no BusinessActor, so the approval action is recorded as 'unspecified'; role recording itself is covered by existing FR09 tests.
- Replacement jobs (V1 -> V2) approving a saved candidate, and concurrent approvals of saved candidates, were not exercised here;
  they use the same DraftApprovalService path that existing F01/F03/AT06 tests cover for scheduler candidates.
- The stale scenarios are produced by changing the isolated test database directly after saving, not through application endpoints.
